# Agents · P · part 24

888 entries, pricing-strategist to proactive. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pricing-strategist**: The Pricing Strategist designs pricing models, business model structure, monetization strategy, and freemium/trial conversion mechanics. - **pricing-strategy-agent**: Researches pricing evidence for one claim: comparable price points, pricing-model structures (seat, usage, one-time, subscription), price-sensitivity data, and the outcomes of observed price changes. Validates the price unit-economics assumes instead of letting it ride as an assumption. Evidence-collector for the Claudify Analyst engine: gathers and returns typed evidence items; never scores beliefs, never recommends a price, never creates files. ([gannino/your-assistant](https://github.com/gannino/your-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pricing-strategy-agent.md))
- **pricing-strategy-agent**: Analyzes competitor pricing, market demand, cost structures, and elasticity to recommend optimal pricing strategies including dynamic, bundle, and promotional models. - **prism-architect**: Winston, the architect. Judges whether an approach fits the codebase at any time, with no plan folder or approval: patterns, data flow, server and client boundaries, coupling, and risk. Answers in chat with a verdict, and returns a fit verdict to a running workflow stage that asks about a scope idea or a challenge finding. Never writes code or a plan file. Triggers: "Winston", architecture, evaluate the approach, is this the right approach, should we, how should I structure, does this pattern fit. ([HunterMcGrew/PRISM](https://github.com/HunterMcGrew/PRISM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prism-architect.md))
- **prism-audit**: Audits the `.prism/` surface for stale content: gives each Decision in a flat plan, a plan folder, or an epic spec a verdict (live, archive-candidate, overdue-archive, open-stale), flags promotion claims whose text is missing from the named file, classifies lessons, flags ADRs and architect docs that have drifted, and writes a report to `.prism/audits/`. Also runs as a subagent for the plan stage's challenge rounds, where its one edit is a round line under ## Challenge.
